============================================================
SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- .next.*
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
     2	import { EB_Garamond } from "next/font/google";
     3	import { ScreenshotPreviewLayer } from "./ScreenshotPreviewLayer";
     4	import "./globals.css";
     5	
     6	const ebGaramond = EB_Garamond({
     7	  subsets: ["latin"],
     8	  style: "italic",
     9	  variable: "--font-eb-garamond",
    10	  weight: "500",
    11	});
    12	
    13	export const metadata: Metadata = {
    14	  metadataBase: new URL(
    15	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || "https://prahl.dev",
    16	  ),
    17	  title: "Prahl.dev | React Native Full Stack Mobile Developer",
    18	  description:
    19	    "Portfolio for Prahl.dev, a React Native full stack mobile developer building polished mobile apps, secure checkout flows, and production-ready Android pipelines.",
    20	  openGraph: {
    21	    title: "Prahl.dev | React Native Full Stack Mobile Developer",
    22	    description:
    23	      "React Native, Next.js, Tailwind CSS, Node.js, and Kotlin mobile portfolio.",
    24	    images: ["/images/start-page-mockup-reference.png"],
    25	  },
    26	};
    27	
    28	export const viewport: Viewport = {
    29	  width: "device-width",
    30	  initialScale: 1,
    31	};
    32	
    33	export default function RootLayout({
    34	  children,
    35	}: Readonly<{
    36	  children: React.ReactNode;
    37	}>) {
    38	  return (
    39	    <html lang="en">
    40	      <body className={ebGaramond.variable}>
    41	        {children}
    42	        <ScreenshotPreviewLayer />
    43	      </body>
    44	    </html>
    45	  );
    46	}

    63	
    64	function StackTerm({
    65	  icon,
    66	  imageAlt,
    67	  imageSrc,
    68	  iconStroke,
    69	  label,
    70	  postmark = false,
    71	  symbol: Symbol,
    72	  symbolLabel,
    73	}: {
    74	  icon?: SimpleIcon;
    75	  imageAlt?: string;
    76	  imageSrc?: string;
    77	  iconStroke?: string;
    78	  label: string;
    79	  postmark?: boolean;
    80	  symbol?: LucideIcon;
    81	  symbolLabel?: string;
    82	}) {
    83	  const iconColor =
    84	    icon?.title === "Gradle"
    85	      ? "#9fd7dc"
    86	      : icon?.title === "PayPal"
    87	        ? "#35bdf2"
    88	      : icon?.hex === "000000" || icon?.hex === "1C2024"
    89	      ? "#f3fff7"
    90	      : icon
    91	        ? `#${icon.hex}`
    92	        : undefined;
    93	
    94	  return (
    95	    <li>
    96	      {imageSrc ? (
    97	        <Image
    98	          alt={imageAlt ?? ""}
    99	          className="capability-brand-mark"
   100	          height={24}
   101	          src={imageSrc}
   102	          width={24}
   103	        />
   104	      ) : postmark ? (
   105	        <svg
   106	          aria-label="Postmark logo"
   107	          className="capability-brand-mark"
   108	          role="img"
   109	          viewBox="0 0 30 30"
   110	        >
   111	          <path
   112	            clipRule="evenodd"
   113	            d="M30 27.422v1.406A1.172 1.172 0 0 0 28.828 30h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0H8.672a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0H4.922a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0H1.172A1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 28.828v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344V8.672a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344V4.922a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344V1.172A1.172 1.172 0 0 0 1.172 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406A1.172 1.172 0 0 0 30 1.172v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344Z"
   114	            fill="#ffde00"
   115	            fillRule="evenodd"
   116	          />
   117	          <path
   118	            d="M8.703 21.38h.935c.481 0 .784-.31.784-.8V9.628c0-.49-.303-.8-.784-.8h-.935V6.4h6.775C19.118 6.4 22 8.286 22 11.876c0 3.616-2.882 5.501-6.522 5.501h-2.326v3.203c0 .49.304.8.81.8h1.895v2.454H8.703V21.38Zm6.573-6.534c2.401 0 3.792-1.033 3.792-2.918 0-1.938-1.39-2.919-3.792-2.919h-2.124v5.863h2.124v-.026Z"
   119	            fill="#000"
   120	          />
   121	        </svg>
   122	      ) : Symbol ? (
   123	        <Symbol
   124	          aria-label={symbolLabel ?? `${label} symbol`}
   125	          className="capability-brand-mark"
   126	          role="img"
   127	        />
   128	      ) : icon ? (
   129	        <svg
   130	          aria-label={`${icon.title} logo`}
   131	          className="capability-brand-mark"
   132	          role="img"
   133	          style={{ color: iconColor }}
   134	          viewBox={iconStroke ? "-1 -1 26 26" : "0 0 24 24"}
   135	        >
   136	          <path
   137	            d={icon.path}
   138	            fill="currentColor"
   139	            paintOrder={iconStroke ? "stroke fill" : undefined}
   140	            stroke={iconStroke}
   141	            strokeLinejoin={iconStroke ? "round" : undefined}
   142	            strokeWidth={iconStroke ? 1.1 : undefined}
   143	          />
   144	        </svg>
   145	      ) : (
   146	        <span className="capability-brand-spacer" aria-hidden="true" />
   147	      )}
   148	      <span>{label}</span>
   149	    </li>
   150	  );
   151	}

   954	function PhonePreview({
   955	  title,
   956	  metric,
   957	  variant,
   958	  href,
   959	  screenImageSrc,
   960	  screenImageAlt,
   961	}: {
   962	  href?: string;
   963	  title: string;
   964	  metric: string;
   965	  variant: "commerce" | "finance";
   966	  screenImageSrc?: string;
   967	  screenImageAlt?: string;
   968	}) {
   969	  const className = `phone-shell phone-shell-${variant}${
   970	    screenImageSrc ? " phone-shell-capture" : ""
   971	  }${href ? " phone-shell-link" : ""}`;
   972	  const content = screenImageSrc ? (
   973	    <Image
   974	      alt={screenImageAlt ?? `${title} app screen`}
   975	      className="phone-framed-image"
   976	      fill
   977	      sizes="245px"
   978	      src={screenImageSrc}
   979	    />
   980	  ) : (
   981	    <>
   982	      <div className="phone-speaker" />
   983	      <div className="phone-screen">
   984	        <div className="phone-status">
   985	          <span>{title}</span>
   986	          <span>5G</span>
   987	        </div>
   988	        <div className="phone-chart">
   989	          <div />
   990	          <div />
   991	          <div />
   992	          <div />
   993	        </div>
   994	        <div className="phone-cardline wide" />
   995	        <div className="phone-cardline" />
   996	        <div className="phone-grid">
   997	          <span />
   998	          <span />
   999	          <span />
  1000	          <span />
  1001	        </div>
  1002	        <div className="phone-cta">{metric}</div>
  1003	      </div>
  1004	    </>
  1005	  );
  1006	
  1007	  if (href) {
  1008	    return (
  1009	      <a
  1010	        aria-label={`Open ${title} case study`}
  1011	        className={className}
  1012	        href={href}
  1013	      >
  1014	        {content}
  1015	      </a>
  1016	    );
  1017	  }
  1018	
  1019	  return <div className={className}>{content}</div>;
  1020	}

    74	function getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset() {
    75	  const isWideLandscape = window.matchMedia(
    76	    "(min-width: 1024px) and (orientation: landscape)",
    77	  ).matches;
    78	  const isPortrait = window.matchMedia("(orientation: portrait)").matches;
    79	
    80	  if (isWideLandscape) {
    81	    return Math.max(18, Math.min(44, window.innerHeight * 0.035));
    82	  }
    83	
    84	  if (isPortrait) {
    85	    return Math.max(14, Math.min(36, window.innerHeight * 0.03));
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  return Math.max(16, Math.min(40, window.innerHeight * 0.0325));
    89	}
    90	
    91	function scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(caseStudyId: string) {
    92	  const targetCard = document.getElementById(caseStudyId);
    93	
    94	  if (!targetCard) {
    95	    return;
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  const caseGrid = targetCard.closest<HTMLElement>(".case-grid");
    99	  const targetTop = targetCard.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   100	  const rowCards = caseGrid
   101	    ? Array.from(caseGrid.children).filter((child): child is HTMLElement => {
   102	        if (!(child instanceof HTMLElement)) {
   103	          return false;
   104	        }
   105	
   106	        const cardTop = child.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   107	
   108	        return Math.abs(cardTop - targetTop) <= 8;
   109	      })
   110	    : [targetCard];
   111	  const rowTop = Math.min(
   112	    ...rowCards.map((card) => card.getBoundingClientRect().top),
   113	  );
   114	
   115	  window.scrollTo({
   116	    behavior: "smooth",
   117	    top: window.scrollY + rowTop - getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset(),
   118	  });
   119	
   120	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", `#${caseStudyId}`);
   121	}

   171	function PhoneMark({
   172	  mobilePlatforms,
   173	}: {
   174	  mobilePlatforms: MobilePlatform[];
   175	}) {
   176	  return (
   177	    <svg
   178	      aria-hidden

   192	      />

   205	          mobilePlatforms.length === 1
   206	            ? "single"
   207	            : index === 0
   208	              ? "top"
   209	              : "bottom";
   210	
   211	        return platform === "android" ? (
   212	          <PhoneAndroidIcon
   213	            key={platform}
   214	            slot={slot === "bottom" ? "top" : slot}
   215	          />
   216	        ) : (
   217	          <PhoneAppleIcon
   218	            key={platform}
   219	            slot={slot === "top" ? "bottom" : slot}
   220	          />
   221	        );
   222	      })}
   223	    </svg>
   224	  );
   225	}
   226	
   227	function TabletMark({
   228	  showPlatformIcon = true,
   229	}: {
   230	  showPlatformIcon?: boolean;
   231	} = {}) {
   232	  return (
   233	    <svg
   234	      aria-hidden
   235	      className="carousel-button-tablet"
   236	      height="1.44em"
   237	      style={{ height: "1.44em", width: "2.16em" }}
   238	      viewBox="0 0 36 24"
   239	      width="2.16em"
   240	    >
   241	      <rect
   242	        className="tablet-mini-frame"
   243	        fill="none"
   244	        height="21.2"
   245	        rx="3"
   246	        stroke="#000"
   247	        strokeWidth="2.2"
   248	        width="33.2"
   249	        x="1.4"
   250	        y="1.4"
   251	      />
   252	      <rect
   253	        className="tablet-mini-screen"
   254	        fill="transparent"
   255	        height="16.8"
   256	        rx="1.4"
   257	        stroke="none"
   258	        width="27.8"
   259	        x="4.8"
   260	        y="3.6"
   261	      />
   262	      <circle
   263	        className="tablet-mini-camera"
   264	        cx="3.2"
   265	        cy="12"
   266	        fill="#000"
   267	        r="0.65"
   268	        stroke="none"
   269	      />
   270	      {showPlatformIcon ? <AndroidLogoImage x={13.26608} y={7.65486} /> : null}
   271	    </svg>
   272	  );
   273	}
   274	
   275	function WebMark({ scale = 1 }: { scale?: number }) {
   276	  const size = `${(1.2 * scale).toFixed(3)}em`;
   277	
   278	  return (
   279	    <svg
   280	      aria-hidden
   281	      className="carousel-button-web"
   282	      height={size}
   283	      style={{ height: size, width: size }}
   284	      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
   285	      width={size}
   286	    >
   287	      <circle className="web-globe-shell" cx="12" cy="12" r="8.2" />
   288	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M3.8 12h16.4" />
   289	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M5.6 8.1h12.8" />
   290	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M5.6 15.9h12.8" />
   291	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M12 3.8c2.2 2.3 3.3 5 3.3 8.2s-1.1 5.9-3.3 8.2" />
   292	      <path className="web-globe-line" d="M12 3.8c-2.2 2.3-3.3 5-3.3 8.2s1.1 5.9 3.3 8.2" />
   293	    </svg>
   294	  );
   295	}
   296	
   297	const platformLabelColumnStart = 3;
   298	
   299	function getPlatformLabelGridColumn(index: number) {
   300	  return platformLabelColumnStart + index * 2;
   301	}
   302	
   303	function getPlatformDividerGridColumn(index: number) {
   304	  return getPlatformLabelGridColumn(index) + 1;
   305	}
   306	
   307	function getPlatformListGridTemplate(columnCount: number) {
   308	  const columns = ["92px", "minmax(8px, 1fr)"];
   309	
   310	  Array.from({ length: columnCount }).forEach((_, index) => {
   311	    columns.push("max-content");
   312	
   313	    if (index < columnCount - 1) {
   314	      columns.push("0px");
   315	    }
   316	  });
   317	
   318	  return columns.join(" ");
   319	}
   320	
   321	function PlatformIconSlot({
   322	  brandMark,
   323	  deviceMark,
   324	  gridColumn,
   325	  gridRow,
   326	  label,
   327	  slotIndex,
   328	}: {
   329	  brandMark?: "android" | "apple" | "web";
   330	  deviceMark?: "phone" | "tablet" | "desktop";
   331	  gridColumn?: number;
   332	  gridRow?: number;
   333	  label: string;
   334	  slotIndex?: number;
   335	}) {
   336	  return (
   337	    <span
   338	      className="case-card-title-platform-slot"
   339	      data-platform-brand-mark={brandMark}
   340	      data-platform-device-mark={deviceMark}
   341	      data-platform-index={slotIndex}
   342	      data-platform-label={brandMark || deviceMark ? "" : label}
   343	      data-platform-row={gridRow}
   344	      style={
   345	        gridColumn && gridRow
   346	          ? {
   347	              gridColumn: String(gridColumn),
   348	              gridRow: String(gridRow),
   349	            }
   350	          : undefined
   351	      }
   352	    >
   353	      {deviceMark ? (
   354	        <FactorDeviceMark device={deviceMark} />
   355	      ) : brandMark === "web" ? (
   356	        <WebMark scale={0.72} />
   357	      ) : brandMark ? (
   358	        <img
   359	          alt=""
   360	          aria-hidden="true"
   361	          className={`case-card-title-platform-brand-mark case-card-title-platform-brand-mark-${brandMark}`}
   362	          height={brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16}
   363	          src={
   364	            brandMark === "android"
   365	              ? "/images/android-robot-head.svg"
   366	              : "/images/apple-logo-official.svg"
   367	          }
   368	          style={{
   369	            height: brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16,
   370	            maxHeight: brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16,
   371	            maxWidth: brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16,
   372	            width: brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16,
   373	          }}
   374	          width={brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16}
   375	        />
   376	      ) : null}
   377	    </span>
   378	  );
   379	}
   380	
   381	function PlatformLabelDivider({
   382	  className,
   383	  gridColumn,
   384	  gridRow,
   385	  kind,
   386	}: {
   387	  className?: string;
   388	  gridColumn?: number;
   389	  gridRow?: number | string;
   390	  kind: "grid" | "item";
   391	}) {
   392	  const dividerClassName = [
   393	    `case-card-title-platform-divider case-card-title-platform-${kind}-divider`,
   394	    className,
   395	  ]
   396	    .filter(Boolean)
   397	    .join(" ");
   398	
   399	  return (
   400	    <span
   401	      aria-hidden="true"
   402	      className={dividerClassName}
   403	      style={
   404	        gridColumn && gridRow
   405	          ? {
   406	              gridColumn: String(gridColumn),
   407	              gridRow: String(gridRow),
   408	            }
   409	          : undefined
   410	      }
   411	    />
   412	  );
   413	}
   414	
   415	function PlatformLabelSlots({
   416	  useDeviceMarks,
   417	  useBrandMarks,
   418	  labels,
   419	  row,
   420	}: {
   421	  useBrandMarks?: boolean;
   422	  useDeviceMarks?: boolean;
   423	  labels: string[];
   424	  row: number;
   425	}) {
   426	  return labels.map((label, index) => (
   427	    <PlatformIconSlot
   428	      gridColumn={getPlatformLabelGridColumn(index)}
   429	      gridRow={row}
   430	      key={`${row}-${label}`}
   431	      label={label}
   432	      brandMark={
   433	        useBrandMarks
   434	          ? label === "AND"
   435	            ? "android"
   436	            : label === "IOS"
   437	              ? "apple"
   438	              : label === "WEB"
   439	                ? "web"
   440	                : undefined
   441	          : undefined
   442	      }
   443	      deviceMark={
   444	        useDeviceMarks
   445	          ? label === "PHONE"
   446	              ? "phone"
   447	            : label === "TABLET"
   448	              ? "tablet"
   449	              : label === "DESK"
   450	                ? "desktop"
   451	                : undefined
   452	          : undefined
   453	      }
   454	      slotIndex={index}
   455	    />
   456	  ));
   457	}
   458	
   459	function PlatformColumnDividers({
   460	  columnCount,
   461	  gridRow,
   462	  partial,
   463	  startIndex = 0,
   464	}: {
   465	  columnCount: number;
   466	  gridRow: number | string;
   467	  partial?: boolean;
   468	  startIndex?: number;
   469	}) {
   470	  return Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, columnCount - 1 - startIndex) }).map(
   471	    (_, index) => {
   472	      const dividerIndex = startIndex + index;
   473	
   474	      return (
   475	        <PlatformLabelDivider
   476	          className={
   477	            partial
   478	              ? "case-card-title-platform-partial-divider"
   479	              : undefined
   480	          }
   481	          gridColumn={getPlatformDividerGridColumn(dividerIndex)}
   482	          gridRow={gridRow}
   483	          key={`divider-${gridRow}-${dividerIndex}`}
   484	          kind="item"
   485	        />
   486	      );
   487	    },
   488	  );
   489	}
   490

   553	function PlatformLogoMark({ platform }: { platform: MobilePlatform }) {
   554	  return platform === "android" ? (
   555	    <AndroidPlatformMark />
   556	  ) : (
   557	    <ApplePlatformMark />
   558	  );
   559	}
   560	
   561	function FactorDeviceMark({
   562	  device,
   563	}: {
   564	  device: "phone" | "tablet" | "desktop";
   565	}) {
   566	  if (device === "phone") {
   567	    return <PhoneMark mobilePlatforms={[]} />;
   568	  }
   569	
   570	  if (device === "tablet") {
   571	    return <TabletMark showPlatformIcon={false} />;
   572	  }
   573	
   574	  return (
   575	    <img
   576	      alt=""
   577	      aria-hidden="true"
   578	      className="case-card-title-desktop-mark"
   579	      src="/images/freeicons-chip-component-white.svg"
   580	    />
   581	  );
   582	}
   583	
   584	export function ProjectDeviceStack({
   585	  assetGap,
   586	  color,
   587	  hasTablet,
   588	  hasWeb,
   589	  deviceLabels,
   590	  mobilePlatforms,
   591	  platformLabels: customPlatformLabels,
   592	  usePlatformBrandMarks,
   593	  responsiveAssetGap,
   594	  showPlatformLabels,
   595	  webIconScale,
   596	}: {
   597	  assetGap?: string;
   598	  color: string;
   599	  deviceLabels?: string[];
   600	  hasTablet?: boolean;
   601	  hasWeb?: boolean;
   602	  mobilePlatforms: MobilePlatform[];
   603	  platformLabels?: string[];
   604	  usePlatformBrandMarks?: boolean;
   605	  responsiveAssetGap?: ResponsiveAssetGap;
   606	  showPlatformLabels?: boolean;
   607	  webIconScale?: number;
   608	}) {
   609	  const stackRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
   610	  const [measuredAssetGap, setMeasuredAssetGap] = useState<string | null>(null);
   611	  const mobilePlatformKey = mobilePlatforms.join(",");
   612	  const stackStyle: CSSProperties & {
   613	    "--project-device-stack-gap"?: string;
   614	  } = {
   615	    color,
   616	  };
   617	  const stackGap = measuredAssetGap ?? assetGap;
   618	  const deviceLabelKey = deviceLabels?.join(",") ?? "";
   619	  const platformLabelKey = customPlatformLabels?.join(",") ?? "";
   620	
   621	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   622	    if (!responsiveAssetGap) {
   623	      setMeasuredAssetGap(null);
   624	      return;
   625	    }
   626	
   627	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   628	    const measurementTarget = stack?.parentElement ?? stack;
   629	
   630	    if (!measurementTarget) {
   631	      return;
   632	    }
   633	
   634	    let frame = 0;
   635	    const updateGap = () => {
   636	      if (frame) {
   637	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   638	      }
   639	
   640	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   641	        const laneWidth = measurementTarget.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   642	        const activeRange = Math.max(
   643	          1,
   644	          responsiveAssetGap.wideLanePx - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx,
   645	        );
   646	        const progress = Math.min(
   647	          1,
   648	          Math.max(
   649	            0,
   650	            (laneWidth - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx) / activeRange,
   651	          ),
   652	        );
   653	        const nextGap =
   654	          responsiveAssetGap.minPx +
   655	          (responsiveAssetGap.maxPx - responsiveAssetGap.minPx) * progress;
   656	        const nextGapValue = `${nextGap.toFixed(2)}px`;
   657	
   658	        setMeasuredAssetGap((currentGap) =>
   659	          currentGap === nextGapValue ? currentGap : nextGapValue,
   660	        );
   661	      });
   662	    };
   663	
   664	    updateGap();
   665	
   666	    const resizeObserver =
   667	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   668	        ? null
   669	        : new ResizeObserver(updateGap);
   670	
   671	    resizeObserver?.observe(measurementTarget);
   672	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   673	
   674	    return () => {
   675	      if (frame) {
   676	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   677	      }
   678	
   679	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   680	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   681	    };
   682	  }, [
   683	    responsiveAssetGap?.maxPx,
   684	    responsiveAssetGap?.minPx,
   685	    responsiveAssetGap?.tightLanePx,
   686	    responsiveAssetGap?.wideLanePx,
   687	  ]);
   688	
   689	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   690	    if (!showPlatformLabels) {
   691	      return;
   692	    }
   693	
   694	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   695	    const rowDivider = stack?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   696	      ".case-card-title-platform-row-divider",
   697	    );
   698	
   699	    if (!stack || !rowDivider) {
   700	      return;
   701	    }
   702	
   703	    let frame = 0;
   704	    const updateRowDivider = () => {
   705	      if (frame) {
   706	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   707	      }
   708	
   709	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   710	        const platformHeading = stack.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   711	          ".case-card-title-platform-heading-platform",
   712	        );
   713	        const deviceHeading = stack.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   714	          ".case-card-title-platform-heading-factor",
   715	        );
   716	        const verticalDividers = Array.from(
   717	          stack.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
   718	            ".case-card-title-platform-divider",
   719	          ),
   720	        );
   721	
   722	        if (
   723	          !platformHeading ||
   724	          !deviceHeading ||
   725	          verticalDividers.length === 0
   726	        ) {
   727	          stack.removeAttribute("data-platform-row-divider-ready");
   728	          return;
   729	        }
   730	
   731	        const stackRect = stack.getBoundingClientRect();
   732	        const platformRect = platformHeading.getBoundingClientRect();
   733	        const deviceRect = deviceHeading.getBoundingClientRect();
   734	        const dividerRects = verticalDividers.map((divider) =>
   735	          divider.getBoundingClientRect(),
   736	        );
   737	        const leftmostDividerCenter = Math.min(
   738	          ...dividerRects.map((rect) => rect.left + rect.width / 2),
   739	        );
   740	        const rightmostDividerCenter = Math.max(
   741	          ...dividerRects.map((rect) => rect.left + rect.width / 2),
   742	        );
   743	        const dividerStartCenter = deviceRect.left + deviceRect.width / 2;
   744	        const dividerOverhang = Math.max(
   745	          0,
   746	          leftmostDividerCenter - dividerStartCenter,
   747	        );
   748	        const dividerEndCenter = rightmostDividerCenter + dividerOverhang;
   749	        const dividerStart = dividerStartCenter - stackRect.left;
   750	        const dividerEnd = dividerEndCenter - stackRect.left;
   751	        const dividerTop =
   752	          (platformRect.top +
   753	            platformRect.height / 2 +
   754	            deviceRect.top +
   755	            deviceRect.height / 2) /
   756	            2 -
   757	          stackRect.top;
   758	        const dividerWidth = Math.max(0, dividerEnd - dividerStart);
   759	
   760	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   761	          "--case-platform-row-divider-left",
   762	          `${dividerStart.toFixed(2)}px`,
   763	        );
   764	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   765	          "--case-platform-row-divider-top",
   766	          `${dividerTop.toFixed(2)}px`,
   767	        );
   768	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   769	          "--case-platform-row-divider-width",
   770	          `${dividerWidth.toFixed(2)}px`,
   771	        );
   772	        stack.toggleAttribute(
   773	          "data-platform-row-divider-ready",
   774	          dividerWidth > 0,
   775	        );
   776	      });
   777	    };
   778	
   779	    updateRowDivider();
   780	    document.fonts?.ready.then(updateRowDivider);
   781	
   782	    const resizeObserver =
   783	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   784	        ? null
   785	        : new ResizeObserver(updateRowDivider);
   786	
   787	    resizeObserver?.observe(stack);
   788	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateRowDivider);
   789	
   790	    return () => {
   791	      if (frame) {
   792	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   793	      }
   794	
   795	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   796	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateRowDivider);
   797	    };
   798	  }, [
   799	    deviceLabelKey,
   800	    hasTablet,
   801	    hasWeb,
   802	    mobilePlatformKey,
   803	    platformLabelKey,
   804	    showPlatformLabels,
   805	    stackGap,
   806	  ]);
   807	
   808	  if (stackGap) {
   809	    stackStyle["--project-device-stack-gap"] = stackGap;
   810	  }
   811	
   812	  if (showPlatformLabels) {
   813	    const platforms: Array<MobilePlatform | "web"> = [];
   814	    const factorDevices: Array<"phone" | "tablet"> = [];
   815	
   816	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("android") || hasTablet) {
   817	      platforms.push("android");
   818	    }
   819	
   820	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("apple")) {
   821	      platforms.push("apple");
   822	    }
   823	
   824	    if (hasWeb) {
   825	      platforms.push("web");
   826	    }
   827	
   828	    if (mobilePlatforms.length > 0) {
   829	      factorDevices.push("phone");
   830	    }
   831	
   832	    if (hasTablet) {
   833	      factorDevices.push("tablet");
   834	    }
   835	
   836	    const platformLabels =
   837	      customPlatformLabels ??
   838	      platforms.map((platform) =>
   839	        platform === "web" ? "WEB" : platform === "android" ? "AND" : "IOS",
   840	      );
   841	    const factorDeviceLabels =
   842	      deviceLabels ??
   843	      factorDevices.map((device) =>
   844	        device === "phone" ? "PHONE" : "TABLET",
   845	      );
   846	    const labelColumnCount = Math.max(
   847	      platformLabels.length,
   848	      factorDeviceLabels.length,
   849	      1,
   850	    );
   851	    const dividerGridRow = factorDeviceLabels.length > 0 ? "1 / 3" : 1;
   852	    const spanningItemDividerCount =
   853	      factorDeviceLabels.length > platformLabels.length
   854	        ? platformLabels.length
   855	        : Math.max(0, platformLabels.length - 1);
   856	    const platformListStyle = {
   857	      ...stackStyle,
   858	      gridTemplateColumns: getPlatformListGridTemplate(labelColumnCount),
   859	    };
   860	
   861	    return (
   862	      <span
   863	        className="carousel-button-project case-card-title-platform-list"
   864	        ref={stackRef}
   865	        style={platformListStyle}
   866	      >
   867	        <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-platform">
   868	          PLATFORM
   869	        </span>
   870	        <PlatformLabelDivider
   871	          gridColumn={2}
   872	          gridRow={dividerGridRow}
   873	          kind="grid"
   874	        />
   875	        <PlatformLabelSlots
   876	          labels={platformLabels}
   877	          row={1}
   878	          useBrandMarks={usePlatformBrandMarks}
   879	        />
   880	        <PlatformColumnDividers
   881	          columnCount={spanningItemDividerCount + 1}
   882	          gridRow={dividerGridRow}
   883	        />
   884	        {factorDeviceLabels.length > 0 ? (
   885	          <>
   886	            <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-factor">
   887	              DEVICE
   888	            </span>
   889	            <PlatformLabelSlots
   890	              labels={factorDeviceLabels}
   891	              row={2}
   892	              useDeviceMarks
   893	            />
   894	            <PlatformColumnDividers
   895	              columnCount={factorDeviceLabels.length}
   896	              gridRow={2}
   897	              partial
   898	              startIndex={spanningItemDividerCount}
   899	            />
   900	            <span
   901	              aria-hidden="true"
   902	              className="case-card-title-platform-row-divider"
   903	            />
   904	          </>
   905	        ) : null}
   906	      </span>
   907	    );
   908	  }
   909	
   910	  return (
   911	    <span className="carousel-button-project" ref={stackRef} style={stackStyle}>
   912	      {mobilePlatforms.map((platform) => (
   913	        <PhoneMark key={platform} mobilePlatforms={[platform]} />
   914	      ))}
   915	      {hasTablet ? <TabletMark /> : null}
   916	      {hasWeb ? <WebMark scale={webIconScale} /> : null}
   917	    </span>
   918	  );
   919	}
   920	
   921	function CarouselArrowMark() {
   922	  return (
   923	    <svg
   924	      aria-hidden
   925	      className="carousel-button-arrow-mark"
   926	      viewBox="0 0 32 24"
   927	      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
   928	    >
   929	      <path
   930	        d="M8.4 12H26M18 4l8 8-8 8"
   931	        fill="none"
   932	        stroke="#000"
   933	        strokeLinecap="round"
   934	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   935	        strokeWidth="6.2"
   936	      />
   937	      <path
   938	        d="M8.4 12H26M18 4l8 8-8 8"
   939	        fill="none"
   940	        stroke="#f3fff7"
   941	        strokeLinecap="round"
   942	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   943	        strokeWidth="3.6"
   944	      />
   945	    </svg>
   946	  );
   947	}
   948	
   949	export function ProjectCarouselButton() {
   950	  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
   951	  const projectButtonRef = useRef<HTMLAnchorElement>(null);
   952	  const activeProject = projectLinks[activeIndex];
   953	
   954	  const handleArrowClick = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
   955	    event.preventDefault();
   956	    scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(activeProject.caseStudyId);
   957	  };
   958	
   959	  useEffect(() => {
   960	    const interval = window.setInterval(() => {
   961	      setActiveIndex((currentIndex) => (currentIndex + 1) % projectLinks.length);
   962	    }, 5000);
   963	
   964	    return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
   965	  }, []);
   966	
   967	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   968	    let frame = 0;
   969	
   970	    const syncHeroActionGeometry = () => {
   971	      if (frame) {
   972	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   973	      }
   974	
   975	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   976	        const heroCopy = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-copy");
   977	        const heroActions =
   978	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
   979	        const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
   980	        const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
   981	        const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
   982	        const phoneStage = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".phone-stage");
   983	        const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
   984	        const profilePhoto =
   985	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
   986	        const socialButton =
   987	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-social-button");
   988	        const visualFrameSelectors = [
   989	          ".profile-photo",
   990	          ".phone-shell-commerce",
   991	          ".phone-shell-finance",
   992	          ".tablet-shell",
   993	        ];
   994	
   995	        if (proofPanel && profilePhoto) {
   996	          const proofRect = proofPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
   997	          const profileRect = profilePhoto.getBoundingClientRect();
   998	          const proofWidth = profileRect.right - proofRect.left;
   999	
  1000	          if (proofWidth > 0) {
  1001	            [heroCopy, proofPanel, stackRow].forEach((element) => {
  1002	              element?.style.setProperty(
  1003	                "--hero-proof-landscape-width",
  1004	                `${proofWidth}px`,
  1005	              );
  1006	            });
  1007	          }
  1008	        }
  1009	
  1010	        if (heroVisual && navBar && stackRow) {
  1011	          const isTwoColumnHero = window.matchMedia(
  1012	            "(min-width: 1041px), (min-width: 981px) and (orientation: landscape)",
  1013	          ).matches;
  1014	
  1015	          if (!isTwoColumnHero) {
  1016	            heroVisual.style.removeProperty("--hero-visual-center-offset");
  1017	          } else {
  1018	            const isCompactLandscapeHero = window.matchMedia(
  1019	              "(min-width: 981px) and (max-width: 1023px) and (orientation: landscape)",
  1020	            ).matches;
  1021	            const heroVisualScale =
  1022	              heroVisual.offsetWidth > 0
  1023	                ? heroVisual.getBoundingClientRect().width / heroVisual.offsetWidth
  1024	                : 1;
  1025	            const deviceStageSnap = isCompactLandscapeHero
  1026	              ? (Number.parseFloat(
  1027	                  window
  1028	                    .getComputedStyle(phoneStage ?? heroVisual)
  1029	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-device-stage-snap"),
  1030	                ) || 0) * heroVisualScale
  1031	              : 0;
  1032	            const visualRects = visualFrameSelectors
  1033	              .map((selector) => ({
  1034	                element: document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector),
  1035	                selector,
  1036	              }))
  1037	              .filter(
  1038	                (
  1039	                  entry,
  1040	                ): entry is { element: HTMLElement; selector: string } =>
  1041	                  Boolean(entry.element),
  1042	              )
  1043	              .map(({ element, selector }) => {
  1044	                const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
  1045	                const snapAdjustment =
  1046	                  selector === ".profile-photo" ? 0 : deviceStageSnap;
  1047	
  1048	                return {
  1049	                  bottom: rect.bottom - snapAdjustment,
  1050	                  top: rect.top - snapAdjustment,
  1051	                };
  1052	              });
  1053	
  1054	            if (visualRects.length > 0) {
  1055	              const navRect = navBar.getBoundingClientRect();
  1056	              const stackRect = stackRow.getBoundingClientRect();
  1057	              const visualTop = Math.min(
  1058	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.top),
  1059	              );
  1060	              const visualBottom = Math.max(
  1061	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.bottom),
  1062	              );
  1063	              const currentOffset =
  1064	                Number.parseFloat(
  1065	                  window
  1066	                    .getComputedStyle(heroVisual)
  1067	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-visual-center-offset"),
  1068	                ) || 0;
  1069	              const visualCenterWithoutOffset =
  1070	                (visualTop + visualBottom) / 2 - currentOffset;
  1071	              const targetCenter = (navRect.bottom + stackRect.top) / 2;
  1072	              const nextOffset = targetCenter - visualCenterWithoutOffset;
  1073	
  1074	              heroVisual.style.setProperty(
  1075	                "--hero-visual-center-offset",
  1076	                `${nextOffset}px`,
  1077	              );
  1078	            }
  1079	          }
  1080	        }
  1081	
  1082	        if (heroActions && socialButton) {
  1083	          const heroActionsWidth = heroActions.getBoundingClientRect().width;
  1084	          const socialButtonHeight =
  1085	            socialButton.getBoundingClientRect().height;
  1086	
  1087	          if (heroCopy && heroActionsWidth > 0) {
  1088	            heroCopy.style.setProperty(
  1089	              "--hero-text-stack-width",
  1090	              `${heroActionsWidth}px`,
  1091	            );
  1092	          }
  1093	
  1094	          if (socialButtonHeight > 0) {
  1095	            const setPx = (name: string, value: number) => {
  1096	              heroActions.style.setProperty(name, `${value}px`);
  1097	            };
  1098	
  1099	            setPx("--hero-social-button-height", socialButtonHeight);
  1100	            setPx("--hero-social-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.295833);
  1101	            setPx("--hero-social-icon-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.672);
  1102	            const baseSocialButtonHeight = 48;
  1103	            const baseSocialGap = baseSocialButtonHeight * 0.045833 + 1;
  1104	            const stretchedSocialGap =
  1105	              Math.max(0, socialButtonHeight - baseSocialButtonHeight) *
  1106	              0.091666;
  1107	
  1108	            setPx("--hero-social-gap", baseSocialGap + stretchedSocialGap);
  1109	            setPx("--hero-social-pad-x", socialButtonHeight / 8);
  1110	            setPx("--hero-view-label-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.275);
  1111	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
  1112	              "--hero-eyebrow-font-size",
  1113	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.367}px`,
  1114	            );
  1115	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
  1116	              "--hero-lede-font-size",
  1117	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.5}px`,
  1118	            );
  1119	            setPx(
  1120	              "--hero-carousel-base-font-size",
  1121	              socialButtonHeight * 0.34375,
  1122	            );
  1123	            setPx(
  1124	              "--hero-carousel-name-font-size",
  1125	              socialButtonHeight * 0.352917,
  1126	            );
  1127	            setPx(
  1128	              "--hero-carousel-category-font-size",
  1129	              socialButtonHeight * 0.176458,
  1130	            );
  1131	            setPx(
  1132	              "--hero-carousel-arrow-font-size",
  1133	              socialButtonHeight * 0.458333,
  1134	            );
  1135	          }
  1136	        }
  1137	
  1138	        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("hero-stack-sync"));
  1139	
  1140	        const viewLabel =
  1141	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
  1142	        const githubText =
  1143	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
  1144	        const githubIcon =
  1145	          document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
  1146	
  1147	        if (viewLabel && githubText && githubIcon) {
  1148	          const viewLabelRect = viewLabel.getBoundingClientRect();
  1149	          const githubTextRect = githubText.getBoundingClientRect();
  1150	          const githubIconRect = githubIcon.getBoundingClientRect();
  1151	          const clusterLeft = Math.min(githubTextRect.left, githubIconRect.left);
  1152	          const stackLeft = clusterLeft - viewLabelRect.left;
  1153	
  1154	          viewLabel.style.setProperty(
  1155	            "--hero-view-stack-left",
  1156	            `${stackLeft}px`,
  1157	          );
  1158	        }
  1159	      });
  1160	    };
  1161	
  1162	    syncHeroActionGeometry();
  1163	    window.addEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1164	
  1165	    const targetIcon =
  1166	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".dribbble-icon-mark") ??
  1167	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".linkedin-icon-mark");
  1168	    const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
  1169	    const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
  1170	    const heroActions = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
  1171	    const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
  1172	    const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
  1173	    const profilePhoto = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
  1174	    const targetButton = targetIcon?.closest(".hero-social-button");
  1175	    const viewLabel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
  1176	    const githubText = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
  1177	    const githubIcon =
  1178	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
  1179	    const githubButton = githubText?.closest(".github-button");
  1180	    const resizeObserver =
  1181	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
  1182	        ? null
  1183	        : new ResizeObserver(syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1184	
  1185	    if (resizeObserver) {
  1186	      if (projectButtonRef.current) {
  1187	        resizeObserver.observe(projectButtonRef.current);
  1188	      }
  1189	
  1190	      if (heroActions) {
  1191	        resizeObserver.observe(heroActions);
  1192	      }
  1193	
  1194	      if (navBar) {
  1195	        resizeObserver.observe(navBar);
  1196	      }
  1197	
  1198	      if (heroVisual) {
  1199	        resizeObserver.observe(heroVisual);
  1200	      }
  1201	
  1202	      if (stackRow) {
  1203	        resizeObserver.observe(stackRow);
  1204	      }
  1205	
  1206	      if (proofPanel) {
  1207	        resizeObserver.observe(proofPanel);
  1208	      }
  1209	
  1210	      if (profilePhoto) {
  1211	        resizeObserver.observe(profilePhoto);
  1212	      }
  1213	
  1214	      if (targetIcon) {
  1215	        resizeObserver.observe(targetIcon);
  1216	      }
  1217	
  1218	      if (targetButton) {
  1219	        resizeObserver.observe(targetButton);
  1220	      }
  1221	
  1222	      if (viewLabel) {
  1223	        resizeObserver.observe(viewLabel);
  1224	      }
  1225	
  1226	      if (githubText) {
  1227	        resizeObserver.observe(githubText);
  1228	      }
  1229	
  1230	      if (githubIcon) {
  1231	        resizeObserver.observe(githubIcon);
  1232	      }
  1233	
  1234	      if (githubButton) {
  1235	        resizeObserver.observe(githubButton);
  1236	      }
  1237	    }
  1238	
  1239	    if ("fonts" in document) {
  1240	      document.fonts.ready.then(syncHeroActionGeometry).catch(() => {});
  1241	    }
  1242	
  1243	    return () => {
  1244	      if (frame) {
  1245	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  1246	      }
  1247	
  1248	      window.removeEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1249	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
  1250	    };
  1251	  }, []);
  1252	
  1253	  return (
  1254	    <span className="carousel-button-group">
  1255	      <a
  1256	        aria-label={`Open ${activeProject.name} project`}
  1257	        className="button button-primary carousel-button"
  1258	        href={activeProject.href}
  1259	        ref={projectButtonRef}
  1260	        rel="noreferrer"
  1261	        target="_blank"
  1262	      >
  1263	        <span className="carousel-button-window" aria-live="polite">
  1264	          <span className="carousel-button-label" key={activeProject.href}>
  1265	            <span
  1266	              className={`carousel-button-action carousel-button-action-${activeProject.tone}`}
  1267	              style={{
  1268	                ...strokedWhiteTextStyle,
  1269	                alignItems: "center",
  1270	                color: projectNameColors[activeProject.tone],
  1271	                display: "inline-flex",
  1272	                flexDirection: "column",
  1273	                fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-name-font-size)",
  1274	                gap: "2.2px",
  1275	                justifyContent: "center",
  1276	                lineHeight: 0.95,
  1277	              }}
  1278	            >
  1279	              <span
  1280	                className="carousel-button-action-name"
  1281	                style={{ fontSize: activeProject.nameFontSize }}
  1282	              >
  1283	                {activeProject.name}
  1284	              </span>
  1285	              <span
  1286	                className="carousel-button-action-category"
  1287	                style={{
  1288	                  WebkitTextStroke: 0,
  1289	                  color: "#000",
  1290	                  fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-category-font-size)",
  1291	                  fontWeight: 900,
  1292	                  letterSpacing: "0.06em",
  1293	                  lineHeight: 1,
  1294	                  textAlign: "center",
  1295	                  textShadow: "none",
  1296	                }}
  1297	              >
  1298	                {activeProject.category}
  1299	              </span>
  1300	            </span>
  1301	            <ProjectDeviceStack
  1302	              color={projectNameColors[activeProject.tone]}
  1303	              hasTablet={activeProject.hasTablet}
  1304	              hasWeb={activeProject.hasWeb}
  1305	              mobilePlatforms={activeProject.mobilePlatforms}
  1306	            />
  1307	          </span>
  1308	        </span>
  1309	      </a>
  1310	      <a
  1311	        aria-label={`Scroll to ${activeProject.name} case study`}
  1312	        className="button button-primary carousel-arrow-button"
  1313	        href={`#${activeProject.caseStudyId}`}
  1314	        onClick={handleArrowClick}
  1315	      >
  1316	        <span aria-hidden className="carousel-button-arrow">
  1317	          <CarouselArrowMark />
  1318	        </span>
  1319	      </a>
  1320	    </span>
  1321	  );
  1322	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Prahl.dev Portfolio Site
     2	
     3	Next.js + Tailwind CSS portfolio start page for Prahl.dev.
     4	
     5	## What is included
     6	
     7	- `app/page.tsx` - the homepage/start page.
     8	- `app/globals.css` - the green/black visual system and responsive layout.
     9	- `public/images/profile-placeholder.png` - generated AI profile placeholder; replace this with your own square photo later using the same filename.
    10	- `public/images/start-page-mockup-reference.png` - the original generated mockup reference.
    11	- `public/favicon.svg` - simple green/black monogram favicon.
    12	
    13	## Local development
    14	
    15	```sh
    16	npm install
    17	npm run dev
    18	```
    19	
    20	Open `http://localhost:3000`.
    21	
    22	## Production build
    23	
    24	For Vercel, Netlify, or another Next.js host:
    25	
    26	```sh
    27	npm run build
    28	```
    29	
    30	For a simple static host:
    31	
    32	```sh
    33	npm run build:static
    34	```
    35	
    36	Then upload the generated `out/` folder.
    37	
    38	## Replace the profile photo
    39	
    40	Put your real square profile photo at:
    41	
    42	```txt
    43	public/images/profile-placeholder.png
    44	```
    45	
    46	Keep the filename the same and rebuild.


============================================================
FILE: DEPLOYMENT.md
============================================================

     1	# Deployment Notes
     2	
     3	## Best Option: Vercel
     4	
     5	1. Upload this `portfolio-site` folder to a GitHub repo.
     6	2. Import the repo in Vercel.
     7	3. Use the default Next.js settings.
     8	4. Build command: `npm run build`.
     9	
    10	## Netlify
    11	
    12	1. Upload this folder to a GitHub repo.
    13	2. Connect the repo in Netlify.
    14	3. Build command: `npm run build`.
    15	4. Publish directory: `.next`.
    16	5. Enable Netlify's Next.js runtime if prompted.
    17	
    18	## Simple Static Hosting
    19	
    20	Use this when the host only accepts HTML/CSS/JS files:
    21	
    22	```sh
    23	npm install
    24	npm run build:static
    25	```
    26	
    27	Upload the contents of:
    28	
    29	```txt
    30	out/
    31	```
    32	
    33	This mode does not need a running Node.js server after upload.
    34	
    35	## Contact Button
    36	
    37	The contact button currently uses:
    38	
    39	```txt
    40	mailto:hello@example.com
    41	```
    42	
    43	Replace `hello@example.com` in `app/page.tsx` with your real email address.

FILE: .github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml
============================================================

     1	name: Build static export for Hostinger
     2	
     3	on:
     4	  push:
     5	    branches: [main]
     6	  workflow_dispatch:
     7	
     8	permissions:
     9	  contents: write
    10	
    11	concurrency:
    12	  group: hostinger-static-export
    13	  cancel-in-progress: true
    14	
    15	jobs:
    16	  build-and-publish:
    17	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    18	    steps:
    19	      - name: Check out source
    20	        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    21	
    22	      - name: Set up Node
    23	        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
    24	        with:
    25	          node-version: 22
    26	          cache: npm
    27	
    28	      - name: Install dependencies
    29	        run: npm ci
    30	
    31	      - name: Build static export
    32	        run: npm run build:static
    33	
    34	      - name: Publish static export branch
    35	        env:
    36	          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    37	        run: |
    38	          cd out
    39	          git init -b hostinger-deploy
    40	          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
    41	          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
    42	          git add -A
    43	          git commit -m "Deploy static export from ${GITHUB_SHA}"
    44	          git remote add origin "https://x-access-token:${GITHUB_TOKEN}@github.com/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}.git"
    45	          git push --force origin hostinger-deploy

============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/portfolio_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Portfolio project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Portfolio Next.js PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you do not understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	10. For this Portfolio repo/website project, always ask me for the commit message before committing or pushing.
    28	
    29	
    30	============================================================
    31	PORTFOLIO PROJECT NOTES
    32	============================================================
    33	
    34	- Main source branch: main
    35	- Deployment source: GitHub Actions builds main into the hostinger-deploy branch
    36	- Hostinger root directory: public_html
    37	- Main stack represented by the website: React / React Native, Next.js, Tailwind CSS, Node.js, Kotlin
    38	- Public website assets live under public/
    39	- Run this script before starting a Portfolio session to refresh the project snapshot.
    40	
    41	
    42	============================================================
    43	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    44	============================================================
    45	
    46	This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    47	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    48	
    49	The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
    50	- .git
    51	- node_modules
    52	- .next
    53	- .next.*
    54	- out
    55	- dist
    56	- build
    57	- .cache
    58	- .turbo
    59	- .npm
    60	- .pnpm-store
    61	- .yarn
    62	- .DS_Store
    63	- next-env.d.ts
    64	- *.tsbuildinfo
    65	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    66	- *.zip
    67	- .env and .env*.local
    68	
    69	Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    70	
    71	
    72	============================================================
    73	PROJECT FILE TREE
    74	============================================================
    75	
    76	EOF
    77	}
    78	
    79	print_file() {
    80	  local file="$1"
    81	
    82	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    83	    {
    84	      echo ""
    85	      echo ""
    86	      echo "============================================================"
    87	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    88	      echo "============================================================"
    89	      echo ""
    90	      nl -ba "$file"
    91	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	  fi
    93	}
    94	
    95	append_project_tree() {
    96	  # Use Git's own ignore rules so the snapshot cannot drift from .gitignore.
    97	  # Include tracked files plus untracked files that Git does not ignore.
    98	  printf '%s\n' "$PROJECT_DIR" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    99	
   100	  git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard \
   101	    | sort \
   102	    | while IFS= read -r relative_file; do
   103	      case "$relative_file" in
   104	        .git/*|node_modules/*|.next/*|.next.*/*|out/*|dist/*|build/*|.cache/*|.turbo/*|.npm/*|.pnpm-store/*|.yarn/*|.DS_Store|*/.DS_Store|*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt|*.zip|.env|.env*.local|next-env.d.ts|*.tsbuildinfo)
   105	          continue
   106	          ;;
   107	      esac
   108	
   109	      printf '%s/%s\n' "$PROJECT_DIR" "$relative_file" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   110	    done
   111	}
   112	
   113	print_inventory_header() {
   114	  local title="$1"
   115	
   116	  {
   117	    echo ""
   118	    echo ""
   119	    echo "============================================================"
   120	    echo "$title"
   121	    echo "============================================================"
   122	    echo ""
   123	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   124	}
   125	
   126	print_file_inventory_line() {
   127	  local file="$1"
   128	  local size
   129	  local modified
   130	
   131	  size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   132	  modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   133	  printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   134	}
   135	
   136	print_dir_inventory() {
   137	  local title="$1"
   138	  local dir="$2"
   139	
   140	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   141	
   142	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
   143	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   144	    return
   145	  fi
   146	
   147	  find "$dir" -type f \
   148	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   149	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   150	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   151	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   152	  done
   153	}
   154	
   155	print_matching_inventory() {
   156	  local title="$1"
   157	  shift
   158	
   159	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   160	
   161	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" "$@" -type f \
   162	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git/*" \
   163	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/node_modules/*" \
   164	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next/*" \
   165	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next.*/*" \
   166	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/out/*" \
   167	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   168	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   169	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   170	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   171	  done
   172	}
   173	
   174	append_git_context() {
   175	  {
   176	    echo ""
   177	    echo ""
   178	    echo "============================================================"
   179	    echo "GIT CONTEXT"
   180	    echo "============================================================"
   181	    echo ""
   182	    echo "Current branch:"
   183	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" branch --show-current 2>/dev/null || true
   184	    echo ""
   185	    echo "Status:"
   186	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" status --short --branch 2>/dev/null || true
   187	    echo ""
   188	    echo "Recent commits:"
   189	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" log --oneline --decorate -10 2>/dev/null || true
   190	    echo ""
   191	    echo "Remotes:"
   192	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" remote -v 2>/dev/null || true
   193	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   194	}
   195	
   196	write_snapshot() {
   197	  write_header
   198	
   199	  append_project_tree
   200	
   201	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   202	
   203	
   204	============================================================
   205	FILE CONTENTS - APP SOURCE
   206	============================================================
   207	
   208	EOF
   209	
   210	  for file in \
   211	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/layout.tsx" \
   212	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/page.tsx" \
   213	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/ProjectCarouselButton.tsx" \
   214	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/globals.css"
   215	  do
   216	    print_file "$file"
   217	  done
   218	
   219	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   220	
   221	
   222	============================================================
   223	FILE CONTENTS - ROOT / TOOLING / DEPLOYMENT
   224	============================================================
   225	
   226	EOF
   227	
   228	  for file in \
   229	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   230	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   231	    "$PROJECT_DIR/DEPLOYMENT.md" \
   232	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package.json" \
   233	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package-lock.json" \
   234	    "$PROJECT_DIR/next.config.ts" \
   235	    "$PROJECT_DIR/postcss.config.mjs" \
   236	    "$PROJECT_DIR/tsconfig.json" \
   237	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml" \
   238	    "$PROJECT_DIR/public/favicon.svg" \
   239	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   240	  do
   241	    print_file "$file"
   242	  done
   243	
   244	  append_git_context
   245	
   246	  print_dir_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY - PUBLIC IMAGES" "$PROJECT_DIR/public/images"
   247	  print_matching_inventory "PDF WORKLOG INVENTORY" \( -name "portfolio_worklog_*.pdf" \)
   248	  print_matching_inventory "DEPLOYMENT ARCHIVE INVENTORY" \( -name "*.zip" \)
   249	
   250	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   251	
   252	
   253	============================================================
   254	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   255	============================================================
   256	
   257	The Portfolio snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private paths:
   258	- .git
   259	- node_modules
   260	- .next
   261	- .next.*
   262	- out
   263	- dist
   264	- build
   265	- .cache
   266	- .turbo
   267	- .npm
   268	- .pnpm-store
   269	- .yarn
   270	- .DS_Store
   271	- next-env.d.ts
   272	- *.tsbuildinfo
   273	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   274	- *.zip
   275	- .env and .env*.local
   276	
   277	The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
   278	
   279	EOF
   280	}
   281	
   282	write_snapshot
   283	
   284	echo "Done."
   285	echo ""
   286	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   287	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   288	echo ""
   289	echo "Open it with:"
   290	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Portfolio snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private paths:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- .next.*
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
